Projection simplex: ensure sum=1. Could use clip to0 and normalize by sum; not exact projection but moves within simplex? If subtract gradient, negative small; clipping and normalizing is a simple projection (not Euclidean but feasible). More robust: implement exact projection:
```
def project_simplex(v):
 v=np.copy(v); s=np.sort(v)[::-1]; css=np.cumsum(s); rho=np.where(s> (1+css)/np.arange(1,len(s)+1)).nonzero()[0][-1]; theta=(1+css[rho])/(rho+1); return np.clip(v-theta,0,None)
```
O(n logn) but for n up to 1024 fine. Could use simple clip+normalize for speed; may lose information if clip large. Use exact if n moderate.

Gradient with LSE weights:
```
def gradient_H(a,w):
    # w length 2n-1 sum1
    ar = a[::-1]
    # Use fftconvolve for speed if scipy.signal available
    conv = fftconvolve(ar, w, mode='full')  # length 3n-2
    n = a.size
    return conv[n-1:2*n-2]  # length n? indices n-1 to 2n-2 inclusive length n
```

Let's enhance search using a known method for minimax convolution: "mutual best response" via solving a sequence of LPs from parent SOTA but with exact objective. Let's understand and perhaps implement a more effective conditional gradient.

Original LP: Given f, M=||f*f||∞, solve max sum b subject to b≥0, ||f*b||∞≤M. This finds a point b in a polytope. If we normalize both to same sum, the new h=(1-t)f+t b. We can choose M such that f in set. Perhaps using it as a response in a zero-sum game can converge to minimax? Consider game: Player1 chooses f, cost max f*f? Player2 chooses b as best response to f with bound M? Not.

But we can use LP to maximize sum under f*b≤M while also b*b≤M? Redundant constraints quadratic not LP. Alternatively solve LP with constraints a*b≤M and b*b≤M? Not LP.

Could use LP to find direction that reduces active constraints: At current a, active set A. We need a perturbation δ with ∑δ=0 and ∑ active grad δ ≤0. This is linear constraints; we can find δ via LP maximizing some norm subject to active derivative≤0. Then update. Our subgradient does that.

Maybe use "alternating algorithm for constant weight difference sets": Start with a, compute c. Set a_i ← max(0, a_i - step * (∑ active c_k grad?) ), then normalize. This is our method.

Let's design `optimize_seq(a0, n, time_limit, rng, lam_list)`:
```
a = normalize(a0)
best = a.copy(); bestT=max(conv(a,a))
mt=np.zeros(n); vt=np.zeros(n)
eta = 0.1 / np.sqrt(n)
beta1=0.9; beta2=0.999
no_improve=0
for it in range(max_iter):
   if time done: break
   c=conv_full(a,a); T=c.max()
   if T < bestT: best=a.copy(); bestT=T; no_improve=0
   else: no_improve+=1
   if no_improve > 50:
       # random restart within local: mix with rng start or binomial?
       # perturb
       pert = a + rng.normal(0, 0.2/np.sqrt(n), n)
       b=project(pert)
       Tb=convfull(b,b).max()
       if Tb < bestT*(1+0.01): a=b; mt*=0.5; vt*=0.5; no_improve=0
       else: # try reducing lambda?
       if no_improve>200: break
   # LSE weights with current lam (cycle)
   lam = lam_list[it % len] * n? scale.
   z=lam*c; z-=z.max(); w=exp(z); w/=w.sum()
   G = 2*grad_conv(a,w)
   # gradient of F (sum1 variables, no need constraint manifold? Project after update)
   mt=...; vt=...
   step = eta * mt/(sqrt(vt)+1e-8)
   # line search factors [1,0.5,2,0.25,4]
   accepted=False
   factors=[1.0,0.5,2.0,0.25,4.0]
   for f in factors:
      b = project(a - step*f)
      cb=conv_full(b,b); Tb=cb.max()
      if Tb < T - 1e-12 or Tb < bestT:
          a=b
          # optionally update best
          if Tb < bestT: best=b.copy(); bestT=Tb
          accepted=True
          break
   if not accepted:
      # try smaller step f=0.1
      ... if still no, nudge: b=project(a - step*0.1); if finite accept even if Tb==T?
```

Alternative: Use `scipy.optimize.minimize` L-BFGS-B on F with y variables. L-BFGS-B can take larger effective steps and converges faster for smooth F. Let's incorporate for low n.
```
def objective_y(y):
   a = softmax(y)  # sum1 nonneg; use stable softmax
   c=conv(a,a)
   F=soft_max(c,lam)
   return F
def grad_y(y):
   a=softmax(y)
   G=gradient c -> same
   return G - a*np.dot(G,a)
```
Softmax normalization may be ill-conditioned if large y; but starts y=log(a+eps). def beta_start(n,a,b):

 w=np.exp(log)

`optimize_sequence(a0, n, max_evals, lam_factor, rng)`:
Use custom projected gradient with LSE. We need balance code.
Pseudo:
```
a=normalize(a0)
c=conv(a,a); best_c=c.max(); best_a=a.copy()
mt=np.zeros(n); vt=np.zeros(n)
lam = lam_factor*n
eta_scale=1.0
for it in range(max_evals//4):
   if time check break
   c=conv(a,a); T=c.max()
   if T < best_c - 1e-12: best_a=a.copy(); best_c=T
   # softmax
   z=lam*c; z-=z.max(); w=np.exp(z); wsum=w.sum(); if not finite: ...
   w/=wsum
   G=2*grad(a,w)
   # adaptive momentum
   mt=0.9*mt+0.1*G
   vt=0.95*vt+0.05*G*G
   u=mt/(np.sqrt(vt)+1e-8)
   # desired update magnitude
   umax=np.max(np.abs(u))
   if umax==0: continue
   target = 0.5 / n * max(0.1, 1.0/umax)?? 
```
Maybe set `u = -u / umax` (direction normalized max1), and choose step `alpha` via line search. Use alpha candidates `[0.05/n, 0.1/n, 0.2/n, 0.5/n, 1.0/n, 2.0/n]`. For each, b=project(a + alpha*u) (u already negative). Evaluate Tb. Accept first with Tb < T (or best_c). If none, maybe accept smallest Tb for diversity if < best_c + 1e-6? Avoid drift.
This is simpler and robust. Direction from LSE gradient normalized. Step size ~1/n to O(1)/n. Good.
Project fast: `b=np.clip(a+alpha*u,0,None); s=b.sum(); if s==0: b=uniform; else b=b/s`.
This uses fast update. For current a, u direction negative; maybe all positive improvements small. We can also try both directions? If no improvement, try plus (opposite) sometimes? def interpolate_seq(a, n):
    m=len(a); if m==n: return a.copy()
    xm=(np.arange(m)+0.5)/m; # normalized density a*m
    ym=a*m
    xn=(np.arange(n)+0.5)/n

```

def propose_candidate(...):
    start=time.monotonic(); deadline=start+max(10,budget_s-60)
    rng=...
    best_a=None; best_sc=inf
    def record(a, sc): ...
    # helper record_np uses score_np.

    # parent
    try parent...
       pareent_norm=normalize(parent); sc=score_np; record
       # maybe evaluate exact later
    # binomial at N
    N = 1024 if budget>600 else 512
    record(central_binomial(N), score_np)
    # beta search
    beta_best = beta_search(N, deadline, rng)
    record(beta_best)
    # local refine best
    if time: local_refine(best_a,N,deadline, trials=200)
    # try upscale
    if time and best_score > 1.51: 
       for N2 in [2048]:
           a2=interp(best_a,N2); record; local_refine(a2,N2,trials=50)
    # final exact validate
    a_out = best_a if finite else central_binomial(1024)
    a_out = np.clip(a_out,0,1000); if sum<0.01: ones
    score=evaluate_sequence([float(x) for x in a_out])
    if not finite or score>best_score+1e-9: # fallback exact binomial
       fb=central_binomial(512); sc=evaluate_sequence(...); if sc<score return fb
    return list
```

```python
import numpy as np
import time
import math

try:
    from scipy.signal import fftconvolve as _fftconvolve
except Exception:
    _fftconvolve = None

try:
    from scipy.optimize import minimize as _scipy_minimize
    from scipy.optimize import linprog as _scipy_linprog
except Exception:
    _scipy_minimize = None
    _scipy_linprog = None


def _conv_full(a, b):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    b = np.asarray(b, dtype=np.float64)
    if a.size == 0 or b.size == 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    if _fftconvolve is not None and max(a.size, b.size) >= 256:
        try:
            out = _fftconvolve(a, b, mode="full").real
            out = np.maximum(out, 0.0)
            return out
        except Exception:
            pass
    out = np.convolve(a, b)
    if out.size:
        out = np.maximum(out, 0.0)
    return out


def _normalize_nonneg(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, None)
    if a.size == 0:
        return np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
        return np.full(a.size, 1.0 / a.size, dtype=np.float64)
    return a / s


def _internal_score(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    if a.size == 0 or not np.isfinite(a).all():
        return float("inf")
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s < 0.01:
        return float("inf")
    c = _conv_full(a, a)
    if c.size == 0:
        return float("inf")
    m = float(np.max(c))
    if not np.isfinite(m):
        return float("inf")
    return float(2.0 * a.size * m / (s * s))


def _central_binomial_seq(n):
    n = int(max(1, n))
    p = np.empty(n, dtype=np.float64)
    p[0] = 1.0
    for i in range(1, n):
        p[i] = p[i - 1] * (2.0 * i - 1.0) / (2.0 * i)
    return _normalize_nonneg(p)


def _beta_seq(n, p, q):
    n = int(max(2, n))
    p = float(p)
    q = float(q)
    if p <= -1.0:
        p = -0.99
    if q <= -1.0:
        q = -0.99
    x = (np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64) + 0.5) / n
    lx = np.log(x)
    ly = np.log(np.maximum(1.0 - x, 1e-300))
    wlog = p * lx + q * ly
    wlog = np.where(np.isfinite(wlog), wlog, -1e300)
    wlog -= np.max(wlog)
    w = np.exp(wlog)
    return _normalize_nonneg(w)


def _rational_seq(n, c):
    n = int(max(2, n))
    x = (np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64) + 0.5) / n
    c = float(max(1e-6, c))
    w = 1.0 / (1.0 + c * x)
    return _normalize_nonneg(w)


def _interpolate_seq(a, n):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    if a.size != int(n):
        m = a.size
        if m <= 1:
            a = np.full(int(n), a[0] if m else 1.0)
            return _normalize_nonneg(a)
        xa = (np.arange(m, dtype=np.float64) + 0.5) / m
        ya = a * m
        xb = (np.arange(int(n), dtype=np.float64) + 0.5) / int(n)
        yb = np.interp(xb, xa, ya)
        a = yb
    return _normalize_nonneg(a)


def _beta_param_search(n, deadline, rng, time_budget):
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    limit = min(deadline, t0 + max(0.5, float(time_budget)))
    best_score = float("inf")
    best_a = None
    best_pq = None

    candidates = []
    # Broad, mildly structured scan.  p is power at 0, q power at 1.
    p_vals = [-0.75, -0.60, -0.45, -0.30, -0.15, 0.0, 0.15, 0.35, 0.60, 0.90, 1.30, 1.70]
    q_vals = [-0.45, -0.25, -0.05, 0.15, 0.45, 0.80, 1.10, 1.50, 1.90]

    for p in p_vals:
        for q in q_vals:
            if time.monotonic() > limit:
                return best_a, best_score, best_pq
            a = _beta_seq(n, p, q)
            sc = _internal_score(a)
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
                best_score = sc
                best_a = a.copy()
                best_pq = (p, q)
            candidates.append((sc, p, q))

    if best_a is None or best_pq is None:
        return _central_binomial_seq(n), _internal_score(_central_binomial_seq(n)), (0.0, 0.0)

    # Local 2-D refinement around the best analytic parameter.
    p0, q0 = best_pq

    def obj(par):
        p, q = float(par[0]), float(par[1])
        if p <= -0.99:
            p = -0.99
        if q <= -0.99:
            q = -0.99
        if p > 3.0 or q > 3.0:
            return 1e8
        return _internal_score(_beta_seq(n, p, q))

    # Deterministic local grid refinement.
    for dp in (-0.25, -0.12, -0.05, -0.02, 0.0, 0.02, 0.05, 0.12, 0.25):
        for dq in (-0.35, -0.18, -0.08, -0.03, 0.0, 0.03, 0.08, 0.18, 0.35):
            if time.monotonic() > limit:
                break
            p = min(3.0, max(-0.99, p0 + dp))
            q = min(3.0, max(-0.99, q0 + dq))
            a = _beta_seq(n, p, q)
            sc = _internal_score(a)
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
                best_score = sc
                best_a = a.copy()
                best_pq = (p, q)
        if time.monotonic() > limit:
            break

    # Optional scipy refinement if available and affordable.
    if _scipy_minimize is not None and (deadline - time.monotonic()) > 8.0:
        try:
            res = _scipy_minimize(
                obj,
                x0=np.array(best_pq, dtype=np.float64),
                method="Nelder-Mead",
                options={"maxiter": 160, "xatol": 1e-4, "fatol": 1e-7},
            )
            if getattr(res, "success", False) or (res.x is not None and np.isfinite(res.fun)):
                p = min(3.0, max(-0.99, float(res.x[0])))
                q = min(3.0, max(-0.99, float(res.x[1])))
                a = _beta_seq(n, p, q)
                sc = _internal_score(a)
                if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
                    best_score = sc
                    best_a = a.copy()
                    best_pq = (p, q)
        except Exception:
            pass

    return best_a, best_score, best_pq


def _direction_basis(n, rng):
    x = (np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64) + 0.5) / n
    b1 = -1.0 + 2.0 * x
    b2 = 3.0 * x * x - (2.0 / 3.0)
    b3 = np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * x)
    b4 = np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * x)
    b5 = x * (1.0 - x) - 0.25
    b6 = (x < 0.5).astype(np.float64) - 0.5
    basis = [b1, b2, b3, b4, b5, b6]
    for b in basis:
        b -= b.mean()
        s = np.max(np.abs(b))
        if s > 0:
            b /= s
    return basis


def _random_smooth_direction(n, rng, basis):
    if rng.random() < 0.35:
        d = _normalize_nonneg(
            np.abs(rng.normal(size=n)) * (rng.random(size=n) < 0.5)
        ).copy()
    else:
        d = rng.normal(size=n)
    k = max(1, n // 96)
    if k > 1 and _fftconvolve is None or k < n:
        try:
            d = np.convolve(d, np.ones(k, dtype=np.float64) / float(k), mode="same")
        except Exception:
            pass
    else:
        try:
            d = _fftconvolve(d, np.ones(k, dtype=np.float64) / float(k), mode="same").real
        except Exception:
            pass
    d = np.asarray(d, dtype=np.float64)
    d -= d.mean()
    s = np.max(np.abs(d))
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0:
        d = basis[rng.integers(0, len(basis))].copy()
    else:
        d /= s
    return d


def _local_refine(a0, n, deadline, rng, max_trials=None, time_budget=220.0):
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    limit = min(deadline, t0 + max(0.5, float(time_budget)))
    n = int(n)
    a = _normalize_nonneg(a0)
    cur_sc = _internal_score(a)
    best_a = a.copy()
    best_sc = cur_sc
    basis = _direction_basis(n, rng)
    alpos = [0.02, 0.05, 0.12, 0.30, 0.80, 2.20]
    if max_trials is None:
        if n <= 256:
            max_trials = 700
        elif n <= 768:
            max_trials = 350
        else:
            max_trials = 160

    fails = 0
    trial = 0
    while trial < max_trials and time.monotonic() < limit:
        trial += 1
        if trial % 7 == 0:
            d = basis[trial % len(basis)].copy()
            if rng.random() < 0.5:
                d = -d
        else:
            d = _random_smooth_direction(n, rng, basis)
            if rng.random() < 0.5:
                d = -d

        best_cand = None
        best_trial_sc = cur_sc
        for sgn in (1.0, -1.0):
            for alp in alpos:
                eta = sgn * alp
                try:
                    b = a * np.exp(eta * d)
                except Exception:
                    continue
                b = np.where(np.isfinite(b), b, 0.0)
                b = np.clip(b, 0.0, None)
                sb = b.sum()
                if sb <= 0:
                    continue
                b = b / sb
                sc = _internal_score(b)
                if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_trial_sc - 1e-12:
                    best_trial_sc = sc
                    best_cand = b
        if best_cand is not None:
            a = best_cand.copy()
            cur_sc = best_trial_sc
            fails = 0
            if cur_sc < best_sc:
                best_sc = cur_sc
                best_a = a.copy()
        else:
            fails += 1
            if fails > 25:
                # Escape: perturb current best and try again.
                d = rng.normal(size=n)
                d -= d.mean()
                s = np.max(np.abs(d))
                if s > 0:
                    d /= s
                for alp in [0.05, 0.2, 0.5, 1.2]:
                    b = a * np.exp(alp * d)
                    b = np.clip(b, 0.0, None)
                    sb = b.sum()
                    if sb <= 0:
                        continue
                    b = b / sb
                    sc = _internal_score(b)
                    if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc:
                        best_a = b.copy()
                        best_sc = sc
                    if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < max(cur_sc, best_sc) + 1e-9:
                        a = b.copy()
                        cur_sc = sc
                        break
                fails = 0
    return best_a, best_sc


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=1300, **kwargs):
    t_start = time.monotonic()
    try:
        budget = float(budget_s)
    except Exception:
        budget = 1300.0
    if not np.isfinite(budget) or budget <= 5.0:
        budget = max(5.0, budget if np.isfinite(budget) else 5.0)

    deadline = t_start + max(2.0, budget - 45.0)
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)

    best_a = None
    best_sc = float("inf")

    def record(a, sc=None):
        nonlocal best_a, best_sc
        if a is None:
            return
        a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
        if a.size == 0 or not np.isfinite(a).all():
            return
        a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
        if sc is None:
            sc = _internal_score(a)
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc:
            best_sc = float(sc)
            best_a = a.copy()

    # Parent construction, if present.  Use it only as another candidate.
    try:
        parent = np.asarray(height_sequence_1, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
        if parent.size > 0 and np.isfinite(parent).all():
            record(parent, _internal_score(parent))
    except Exception:
        parent = None

    # Guaranteed simple baseline central binomial sequence.
    base_n = 1024 if budget > 700 else 512
    record(_central_binomial_seq(base_n))

    # Primary parametric search.
    n1 = 1024 if budget > 700 else 512
    try:
        a_beta, sc_beta, _ = _beta_param_search(
            n1,
            deadline,
            rng,
            time_budget=min(140.0, max(8.0, (deadline - time.monotonic()) * 0.45)),
        )
        if a_beta is not None:
            record(a_beta, sc_beta)
    except Exception:
        pass

    # Also try a rational family and its interpolation if time remains.
    if best_a is not None and time.monotonic() < deadline - 60.0:
        for c in (0.5, 1.0, 2.0, 5.0):
            a = _rational_seq(n1, c)
            record(a)
            if time.monotonic() >= deadline - 60.0:
                break

    # Local shape refinement on the best candidate found so far.
    if best_a is not None and time.monotonic() < deadline - 40.0:
        nref = best_a.size
        if nref > 2048:
            a_ref = _interpolate_seq(best_a, 2048)
        elif nref < 384:
            a_ref = _interpolate_seq(best_a, 384)
        else:
            a_ref = best_a.copy()
        try:
            a_loc, sc_loc = _local_refine(
                a_ref,
                nref,
                deadline,
                rng,
                time_budget=min(
                    160.0,
                    max(6.0, (deadline - time.monotonic()) * 0.55),
                ),
            )
            record(a_loc, sc_loc)
        except Exception:
            pass

    # Refine a separate binomial start; sometimes helps if beta search is local.
    if time.monotonic() < deadline - 30.0:
        nb = 1024 if n1 >= 1024 else 512
        try:
            a_bin, sc_bin = _local_refine(
                _central_binomial_seq(nb),
                nb,
                deadline,
                rng,
                time_budget=min(80.0, max(4.0, (deadline - time.monotonic()) * 0.5)),
            )
            record(a_bin, sc_bin)
        except Exception:
            pass

    # If still not convincing, upscale the best candidate and do a short polish.
    if (
        best_a is not None
        and np.isfinite(best_sc)
        and best_sc > 1.503
        and time.monotonic() < deadline - 60.0
    ):
        n2 = 2048 if budget > 800 else 1024
        if best_a.size < n2:
            try:
                a_up = _interpolate_seq(best_a, n2)
                record(a_up)
                a_pol, sc_pol = _local_refine(
                    a_up,
                    n2,
                    deadline,
                    rng,
                    max_trials=80,
                    time_budget=min(70.0, max(4.0, deadline - time.monotonic() - 25.0)),
                )
                record(a_pol, sc_pol)
            except Exception:
                pass

    # Sometimes direct low-length exact score can be lower than a very long
    # discretized candidate; scan a few natural lengths on the best shape.
    if best_a is not None and time.monotonic() < deadline - 25.0:
        for cand_n in (512, 768, 1024, 1536):
            try:
                a_try = _interpolate_seq(best_a, cand_n)
                record(a_try)
            except Exception:
                pass

    # Final fallbacks and exact validation.
    if best_a is None or not np.isfinite(best_sc):
        best_a = _central_binomial_seq(1024 if budget > 500 else 512)
        best_sc = _internal_score(best_a)

    final = np.asarray(best_a, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    final = np.clip(final, 0.0, 1000.0)
    if final.size == 0:
        final = np.ones(512, dtype=np.float64)
    s = float(np.sum(final))
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s < 0.01:
        final = _central_binomial_seq(512)

    exact_final = float("inf")
    try:
        exact_final = float(evaluate_sequence([float(x) for x in final]))
    except Exception:
        exact_final = float("inf")

    # If exact evaluator somehow rejects our internal best, fall back to robust
    # simple sequences and let the evaluator choose the best.
    if not np.isfinite(exact_final) or exact_final > max(2.0, best_sc + 1e-6):
        fallbacks = [
            _central_binomial_seq(512),
            _central_binomial_seq(1024),
            _beta_seq(512, -0.30, 1.0),
            _beta_seq(768, -0.35, 1.1),
        ]
        if parent is not None and parent.size > 0:
            fallbacks.append(_interpolate_seq(parent, 512))
        for fb in fallbacks:
            fb = np.clip(np.asarray(fb, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1), 0.0, 1000.0)
            try:
                scf = float(evaluate_sequence([float(x) for x in fb]))
            except Exception:
                scf = float("inf")
            if np.isfinite(scf) and scf < exact_final:
                final = fb
                exact_final = scf

    if best_a is not None and np.isfinite(best_sc) and best_sc < exact_final:
        # consistency check only; exact_final may be slightly worse due to
        # interpolation/environment.  Return the one with better internal score
        # provided it remains valid.
        pass

    return [float(x) for x in final]
```
